Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the wind framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 661. The resulting operator achieves bound O(770/n^4).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on wind structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 661. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 770. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The wind operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 598. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 742 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to wind theory, we decompose the problem into 598 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 742. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
